## Folder summaries

- **02_EVIDENCE**: The 02_EVIDENCE folder contains documentation related to audit processes, evidence gaps, dependency maps, and experiment provenance, all aimed at ensuring data integrity, resolving conflicts, and closing gaps in numerical claims for the Sensors family. (role: governance; key: AUDIT_AND_CONFLICT_REGISTER.md, CLAIM_EVIDENCE_GAPS.md, ANALYSIS_DEPENDENCY_MAP.md, EXPERIMENT_PROVENANCE.md, CLAIM_EVIDENCE_GAPS.md)
- **02_EVIDENCE/COMMON_CONTEXT**: This folder contains a collection of documents that summarize research status, implementation evidence, and audit-related artifacts for a PhD project focused on structural health monitoring. It includes evidence indices, unresolved issues, and summaries of scientific guardrails and provenance. (role: mixed; key: EVIDENCE_INDEX.md, VERIFIED_RESULTS_SUMMARY.md, SCIENTIFIC_GUARDRAILS.md, UNRESOLVED_SCIENTIFIC_ISSUES.md, RESEARCH_CONTEXT.md)
- **02_EVIDENCE/PACKAGE_DOCS**: This folder contains documentation related to multi-camera systems for structural displacement measurement, including research questions, dissertation outlines, policy investigations, measurement scopes, and reproducibility instructions. (role: manuscript; key: CLAIMED_CONTRIBUTIONS_AND_RQ.md, DISSERTATION_CHAPTER_OUTLINE.md, P1_BIBLIOGRAPHIC_RECORD.md, REFERENCES_EXTRACTED.md, REPRODUCIBILITY_INSTRUCTIONS.md)
- **05_TARGETED_A_BLOCKERS**: This folder contains audit and governance documents related to the manuscript submission, focusing on administrative issues, reproducibility, and ensuring no scientific blockers exist. It includes decisions on authorship, data availability, and confirms the manuscript is current and unmodified. (role: governance; key: TARGETED_A_BLOCKER_AUDIT.md, OWNER_DECISIONS.md, NUMERICAL_REPRODUCIBILITY.md, TIMESTAMP_PROVENANCE.md, P1_P2_OVERLAP_MATRIX.md)
- **05_TARGETED_A_BLOCKERS/scripts**: This folder contains scripts for auditing and validating timestamp data, re-implementing estimators, and regenerating spectral data from documented protocols. It focuses on timing sensitivity, data comparison, and spectral analysis. (role: comparison; key: bag_timestamp_audit.py, timestamp_sensitivity.py, verify_s4_windowed.py, verify_s5_background.py, verify_sweep_independent.py)
- **06_CLAUDE_EVIDENCE_AUDIT_2026-10-11**: This folder contains audit and enhancement documents for a paper submission, focusing on issues like evidence gaps, submission blockers, and novelty comparisons. It includes files related to manuscript governance, spectral analysis, and reproducibility improvements. (role: manuscript; key: PAPER2_CLAUDE_EVIDENCE_AUDIT.md, PAPER2_ENHANCEMENT_PRIORITY_REGISTER.md, PAPER2_NOVELTY_AND_OVERLAP_MATRIX.md, PAPER2_SUBMISSION_BLOCKERS.md)
- **_work**: This folder contains multiple documents detailing the limitations of evidence in research and scientific audits, focusing on multi-model audits, verified consistency checks, and the absence of raw data. It includes various versions of audit reports and their associated findings. (role: manuscript; key: CHATGPT_RESEARCH_MENTOR_v3_EVIDENCE_LIMITATIONS.md, CLAUDE_SCIENTIFIC_AUDIT_v2_EVIDENCE_LIMITATIONS.md, GEMINI_RESEARCH_INTELLIGENCE_v3_EVIDENCE_LIMITATIONS.md, CLAUDE_SCIENTIFIC_AUDIT_EVIDENCE_LIMITATIONS.md, CHATGPT_RESEARCH_MENTOR_EVIDENCE_LIMITATIONS.md)
- **_work/stage/CHATGPT_RESEARCH_MENTOR**: This folder contains research files for a PhD student's mentorship package, focusing on AI integration, audit limitations, manuscript preparation, and governance. It includes guidance on avoiding repetition, ensuring defensible submissions, and identifying risks in research. (role: governance; key: AI_TASK_INSTRUCTIONS.md, EVIDENCE_LIMITATIONS.md, START_HERE.md, PACKAGE_README.md, manuscript)
- **_work/stage/CHATGPT_RESEARCH_MENTOR/12_SENSORS_SUBMISSION_MATERIALS**: This folder contains materials for a manuscript submission to *Sensors*, focusing on spectral analysis of wind-tunnel bridge-deck data using per-camera timebases, emphasizing timing, provenance, and spectral features in optical measurements. (role: manuscript; key: Short_Cover_Summary.md, Highlights.md, Novelty_Contribution_Statement.md, Submission_Checklist_MDPI_Sensors.md, Cover_Letter_MDPI_Sensors.md)
